<meta>This is the title page of an alchemical and medical work from 1739. The author uses the pseudonym "Hermogenes," a common practice in the 18th century to protect the identity of an alchemist. The text highlights the dual nature of alchemy: the "human" path (medicine for the body) and the "metallic" path (transmutation).</meta>

# **Preface.**

## Kind Reader!

There has perhaps never been a time more fertile with fraudulent alchemists <note>original: *Alchymisten*. In 1739, "alchemy" was in a state of transition; while it led to the birth of modern chemistry, it was also associated with "gold-makers" and confidence men who cheated the public.</note> than the present. All the bookstores are filled with such books; everywhere one encounters laboratories <note>original: *Laboratoria*</note>, both secret and public; and there is almost no social class <note>original: *Stand*. This refers to the rigid social hierarchy of the time, from the peasantry and craftsmen to the nobility.</note> in which people afflicted with this contagious plague <note>original: *ansteckenden Seuche*. The author uses medical metaphor to describe the "gold-fever" or alchemical craze gripping society.</note> cannot be found. I call it a contagious plague because it is almost unbelievable how far this evil has spread. People complain about these wretched times, about the decline of...

<meta>The catchword at the bottom, "Nah-", indicates the first word of the next page is "Nahrung," likely referring to the "decline of sustenance" or "livelihood."</meta>

<summary>The author introduces his work by lamenting the "plague" of fraudulent alchemy that has infected every level of society and flooded the book market with deceptive texts.</summary>
<keywords>alchemy, fraud, bookstores, laboratories, social classes, 18th century, Hermogenes, public health metaphor</keywords>

# **Preface.**

These are the traditional writings and maxims of those who successfully achieved the Great Work of the Philosopher's Stone.</note> come true to the very letter; whereas, on the other hand, those who are accustomed to working with the wrong <term>matter</term> <note>Matter: The starting substance or "materia prima." Choosing the wrong material was considered the most common reason for failure.</note> do not get to see the <term>Philosophical Heaven</term> <note>Philosophical Heaven: A metaphorical term describing a specific stage of clarity or a certain visual phenomenon (like a starry appearance) occurring inside the alchemical flask.</note>, nor what occurs within it; therefore, out of an impulse for the truth and out of love to help those who are erring find the right path, I have clearly described the work from beginning to end with all <term>precautions</term> <note>original: *Cauteln*. This refers to specific technical warnings or procedural safeguards necessary to avoid ruining the experiment.</note>, in such a way as no single *Philosopher* <note>original: *Philosophus*. ...will be described. And although the **matter** <note>original: *Materie*. The physical substance or "first matter" used as the starting point for the alchemical Work.</note> might be known to some, there still remains a great bolt before the door of the **Art** <note>original: *Kunst*. A traditional term for Alchemy, often called the "Royal Art."</note>, because not only does much depend on the **preliminary work** <note>original: *Vor-Arbeit*; the initial cleaning, grinding, or preparation of the raw materials.</note>, but during the **subsequent work** <note>original: *Nach-Arbeit*; the refining, heating, and transformative stages of the process.</note> all **phenomena** must be observed with the utmost precision. Without **instruction** <note>original: *Communication*; the direct sharing of secrets from a master to a student.</note>, it is unlikely that anyone would discover these things; here, however, they are revealed in the most exact way—not merely compiled from other books, but as **practical experience** <note>original: *Praxis*.</note> itself discloses them. Thus, the truth, which is otherwise very rarely found in such books, may be encountered here without many useless words.

Should anyone desire to make a trial of my ***Spagyric Medicine*** <note>original: *Medicina Spagyrica*. Remedies created using alchemical techniques to separate, purify, and recombine the "essential" parts of a substance.</note> in the most dangerous **diseases**, such can be obtained through the **publisher** of these few pages, along with further information regarding its use. Should there also be **Sons of Art** <note>original: *Filii artis*. A fraternal term for dedicated alchemical practitioners and students.</note> who are convinced by the **perception** <note>original: *Sension*; the intuitive or sensory realization of alchemical truth.</note> of this **secret**—

# EXPLANATION
# OF THE PHILOSOPHICAL TABLE. <note>original: *EXPLICATIO TABULÆ PHILOSOPHICÆ*</note>

### I.

This Hieroglyphic Table, with its figures and characters, is said to have been cast from <term>philosophical gold</term> <note>Unlike common bullion, "philosophical gold" refers to the perfected state of matter and soul sought by alchemists.</note>, and—containing within itself all truths and operations of natural philosophy and the <term>art of the golden flow</term> <note>original: *Kunst des güldenen Flüſſes*; a poetic term for the alchemical process of refining metals or the "Golden Stream" of wisdom.</note>—is reported to have been seen in Isfahan, the great capital and ancient royal residence of Persia, within the inner treasury. This table explains the entire art of metallic transmutation, namely:

2. At the peak of the *Triangle* <note>original: *Trianguli*</note> appears *Mercury* <note>original: *Mercurius*</note> with the double serpent-staff <note>The Caduceus, a symbol of commerce and heralds, but in alchemy, it represents the reconciliation of opposing forces (sulfur and mercury).</note>. This *Mercury* is not the <term>living mercury</term> <note>original: *Mercurius vivus*; common liquid quicksilver.</note> or the *running metallic* mercury, such as common and purified quicksilver; rather, it is a *spirit giving life to all things and a fundamental solvent* <note>original: *Spiritus omnia vivificans & radicale solvens*</note>, the *living agent in the great physical work* <note>original: *Vivum agens in magno opere physico*</note>, and the *universal do-all in the philosophy of the universe* <note>original: *fac totum in Philosophia universi*</note>. He is, so to speak, the master craftsman in all *physical operations* <note>original: *Operationibus physicis*</note> and imparts the highest *virtues* and *qualities* <note>original: *Virtutes* and *Qualitates*</note> to all pure *sublunary subjects and individuals* <note>original: *Subjectis & Individuis sublunaribus*; referring to all earthly things existing below the sphere of the moon.</note> during his operation. This heavenly spirit reveals himself in the Hesperian Garden of Delights <note>A mythological garden at the edge of the world; in alchemy, it symbolizes the place where the "Golden Apples" or the Philosopher's Stone are found.</note> like a pure and beautiful maiden in her heavenly-

<meta>catchword: shining- <note>original: *glän-*, likely for *glänzend* (shining/gleaming).</note></meta>

# EXPLANATION

...shining and green garment of hope, which reveals itself to the <term>Sons of the Art</term> <note>original: *Filiis artis*; those initiated into the secrets of alchemy.</note> as those lovers of this heavenly <term>Philosophy</term> <note>original: *Philosophie*; here referring to the "Divine Art" of alchemy.</note> deemed worthy by God. She reveals to them the golden book of natural wisdom, which she lets fall into the noble <term>fountain</term> <note>original: *Fontina*.</note> or bath. This book is the <term>foliated earth</term> <note>original: *geblätterte Erde*; a term for the matter of the "Great Work" when it begins to calcine or layer.</note> of the Sages; she herself also enters into this secret sweat-bath and, through its gentle and sweet warmth, falls asleep and is <term>entirely</term> <note>original: *in totum*.</note> <term>transformed</term> <note>original: *mutiret*.</note> into a rose-colored blood.

In the Art, this is called the rose-colored blood of the Lion, and it is the <term>Mercury of the Sages</term> <note>original: *Mercurius der Weisen*.</note>: a heavenly, pure spirit through which the entire earth turns green and all of nature is animated by all the <term>celestial stars</term> <note>original: *Astris cœlestibus*.</note>. This spirit reveals itself visibly in the spring with its green color and various flowers—in grass, herbs, foliage, and trees—but in summer and autumn it is seen in its <term>ripening</term> <note>original: *Maturation*.</note> and magnificent fruits.

However, because this spirit—in its color and greenness as its heavenly essence—cannot easily be <term>separated</term> from these <term>vegetable matters</term> <note>original: *Vegetabilibus*; referring to the plant kingdom.</note>, nor can it be maintained pure and <term>by itself</term> <note>original: *per se*.</note> in <term>bodily existence</term> <note>original: *existentia corporali*.</note>, the <term>Philosopher</term> <note>original: *Philosophus*.</note> must seek it in the earth, specifically in the <term>mineral kingdom</term> <note>original: *regno minerali*.</note>. There, God has <term>concentrated</term> it and created it <term>physically</term>, though it remains mixed with many <term>impurities</term> <note>original: *Impuritäten*.</note> in the <term>form</term> <note>original: *forma*.</note> of a salt. Place this into its purification, and lead it through all the <term>spheres</term> of the seven <term>planets</term> <note>original: *Planeten*.</note>...

# PHILOSOPHICAL TABLES. 11

...[refined] toward its highest purification and heavenly beauty and spirituality, like a fire-flame that no longer possesses anything corporeal, but rather—**entirely** <note>original: *in totum*.</note> like **common mercury** <note>original: *☿ vulgi* or *Mercurius vulgi*; ordinary quicksilver.</note>—smokes away in an open fire. This is the **sign of purity** <note>original: *Signum puritatis*.</note> of a philosophically purified, heavenly salt. From this salt, extract the spirit of light in the gentlest warmth—not with retorts, alembics, helms, and receivers, which are all sophisticated and deceptive, but **in the hidden physical egg and in a spagyric manner** <note>original: *in ovo physico occulto & spagyrico modo*; "spagyric" refers to the alchemical process of separating and then recombining components.</note>. There, a pure heavenly spirit will reveal itself, which is our **Mercury** <note>original: *Mercurius*.</note> and the spirit of light, which contains within itself the **introduction of a better form for all things** <note>original: *Introductionem melioris formæ omnium rerum*.</note> and **transforms** <note>original: *mutiret*.</note> the **sublunary individuals** <note>original: *Individua Sublunaria*; entities existing in the world beneath the moon.</note> into its own high nature through the artist’s skill and handiwork. Enclose this pure spirit **hermetically** in a **physical egg** <note>original: *Ovum physicum*.</note> or a vial-glass with a narrow neck, and let it **putrefy** <note>original: *putrificiren*; to undergo decomposition, the first essential stage of the Great Work.</note> and die for 42 days in gentle warmth. Thus, after the elapsed time, it will **awaken** <note>original: *evigiliren*.</note> of its own accord and rise gloriously, and it will perform all the **wonderful physical operations** <note>original: *Operationes physicas mirabiles*.</note> itself; it will accomplish everything described about it in this noble little treatise. Through the help of art and nature, and without further manual labor, it will reach its **ripening** <note>original: *Maturation*.</note> and take on an **incorruptible body** <note>original: *Corpus incorruptibile*.</note> which passes through closed doors and, as a **pure essence of nature** <note>original: *Purum Naturæ*.</note>, concentrated...

# EXPLANATION

...and fixed heavenly spirit, penetrates stone and rock, also steel and iron, along with all metals, and transforms them into its glorious and heavenly essence, as if to its final perfection and supernatural beauty. So much for this **Mercury** <note>original: *Mercurio*.</note>. Now follows the emblem of the snakes in our illustrated Philosophical Table; this snake represents the **Azoth** <note>original: *Azoth*; the universal medicine or "first matter" of the alchemists.</note> of the Sages, our **sharpest vinegar** <note>original: *Acetum acerrimum*; a term for the powerful acidic solvent used to break down metals.</note>, which is the beginning of the Great Work and a fixed **spirit** <note>original: *Spiritus*.</note> and **eternal liquid** <note>original: *Liquor æternus*.</note>, namely our **philosophical Mercury** <note>original: *Mercurius philosophicus*.</note> described above. When enclosed in the **physical egg** <note>original: *ovo physico*; the sealed glass flask where the alchemical transformation occurs.</note> in its watery form, this [mercury] is compared to a snake, which brings nothing but poison and harm to the ignorant and the sophists <note>The author uses "sophists" to describe fraudulent or amateur alchemists who lack true understanding.</note> through the over-hasty use of fire and lack of manual skill. However, this snake leads the **Sons of the Art** <note>original: *Filios artis*; the initiated practitioners of alchemy.</note> into the caverns of riches and gardens of delight and joy, which are obtained through skill in the knowledge of nature and the **governance of fire** <note>original: *Regimine ignis*.</note>.

3. Next follows the winged dragon; this Hesperian dragon <note>A reference to the dragon that guarded the golden apples in the Garden of the Hesperides in Greek mythology; in alchemy, it represents the volatile spirit that must be "tamed" or fixed.</note> represents **Mercury** <note>indicated by the symbol ☿</note> in its rising and sublimating power and force. As the **soul** <note>original: *Anima*.</note> and red soul of the body, it rises through the blackness of **decay** <note>original: *Putrefaction*; the *nigredo* or black stage, the first phase of the Great Work.</note> and the **Raven's Head** <note>original: *Raben-Haupt*; also known as *Caput Corvi*, a symbolic term for the pitch-black residue at the bottom of the flask.</note> like a fire-flame, red as blood; therefore, it is the fiery throat of the Hesperian...

### PHILOSOPHICAL TABLES.

<margin>||</margin> [Hesper]ian dragon <note>The text completes the word "Hesperischen" from the previous page, referring to the dragon that guarded the golden apples in the Garden of the Hesperides.</note>, which is also called the **Dawn of the Sages** <note>original: *Morgenröthe der Weisen*; a poetic term for the beginning of the enlightenment or the successful start of the alchemical process.</note>; here the **soul separates from the physical body** <note>original: *Anima a corpore physico*</note> with great power and **to singular admiration** <note>original: *cum singulari admiratione*</note>. This dragon must by no means be irritated by the fire, but must only receive the **degree of the gentlest boiling in the fire** <note>original: *Gradum lenissimæ decoctionis in igne*</note>; otherwise, it will resist you and the fire itself, and will ruin you along with your ignorance and the entire operation. He who does not get to see this dragon in his operation has no hope of success in all eternity.

4. The **Raven** <note>original: *Raab* (archaic German *Rabe*). The raven is the traditional symbol for the *Nigredo* or "blackening" stage of alchemy.</note> represents the blackness or **putrefaction** <note>The process of breaking down matter so it can be reborn.</note> **in the great physical work** <note>original: *in magno opere physico*</note>. When this blackness separates after 42 days through the very gentlest **degree of boiling** <note>original: *Gradum decoctionis*</note> and settles like a black cap in the **head of the physical egg** <note>original: *capitellum ovi physici*; the top part of the glass flask used in the experiment.</note>, then this blackness is called the **Raven’s Head** <note>original: *Caput Corvi*</note>, and not the **Raven’s Body** <note>original: *Corpus Corvi*</note>, which was the putrefaction itself. However, this decayed Raven has the **balsam of life** hidden within it; for in this putrefaction the true spirits of life gather, which afterwards act with great power. Through the **radical solution** <note>A thorough dissolving of the substance to its very roots or essence.</note> and the highest refinement **by ascent and descent** <note>original: *per ascensionem et descensionem*; referring to the process of evaporation and condensation within the flask.</note>, they make the **physical body** <note>original: *Corpus physicum*</note> heavenly and bring it into **regeneration**. This regeneration is the foundation and the noblest **basis** of the entire Art, for without this putrefaction, no regeneration can arise.

<summary>This page describes the Hesperian dragon and the Raven as symbols for the critical "putrefaction" stage of the alchemical process, where matter must "die" and turn black before it can be regenerated into a heavenly state.</summary>
<keywords>Hesperian dragon, Dawn of the Sages, Raven, Putrefaction, Raven’s Head, Nigredo, Physical Egg, Regeneration, Alchemy, Great Work</keywords>

[putrefac]tion, and without **regeneration** <note>original: *Regeneration*; in alchemy, this refers to the spiritual and physical rebirth of matter after it has been "killed" or broken down.</note> no **body** <note>original: *Corpus*</note> can become **perfect** and heavenly. This is to be especially noted in this art, and it is the **highest good** <note>original: *summum bonum*</note> contained within it.

For after the **Star of Saturn** <note>original: *Astrum Saturni*; associated with lead and the dark, heavy stage of the work</note> has **dried out** <note>original: *exsicciret*</note> and **coagulation** <note>original: *Coagulation*; the process where the liquid matter begins to thicken and solidify</note> has occurred, then the **Star of Jupiter** <note>original: *Astrum Jovis*; representing the transition from blackness to light</note> rises, and the **chaos** <note>original: *Chaos*; the unformed, primal matter of the alchemist</note> becomes white. For after our swan has sunk down again and reached the bottom of the vessel, the **philosophical tree** arises in the **Hesperian Paradise** <note>A mythical garden; the tree represents the growth of the alchemical tincture</note> with its white and matured wondrous fruits. Then the **fixed physical moon** <note>original: *Luna physica fixa*; a stable form of the white tincture</note> and white heavenly queen is born, who restores life and health to all the sick, and gives back strength and appearance to all the old and deformed. She imparts her power to all metals and **improves** <note>original: *melioriret*</note> them, and shares her brilliance and virtue with all stones and **opaque subjects** <note>original: *Subjectis opacis*; materials that do not let light through, which the tincture can make translucent or "noble".</note>.

<meta>catchword: lost</meta>

...his admonition, *make haste slowly* <note>original Latin: *festina lente*; a classic alchemical paradox advising the artist to work with patient deliberation</note>, that one should in no way rush in this work, which is surely the most subtle and cautious warning in the entire operation. One must always pay attention to this and be mindful that Nature is not overtaken, nor overdone with the fire, for she is the wise mistress whom the artist must follow in all operations if he does not wish to harvest harm and loss, and have wasted costs and labor. Therefore, the artist must understand the philosophical <term>Ether</term> <note>the subtle, celestial substance or "spirit of the world" that permeates the universe</note> and observe the <term>physical Heaven</term> <note>original Latin: *Cœlum physicum*; the internal environment of the alchemical vessel where the elements interact</note>, beneath which the pure and heavenly elements act, so that no <term>unnatural heat</term> <note>original Latin: *Calor præter naturam*; heat that is too intense and violates the natural growth cycles of the matter</note> penetrates inside and drives out the <term>living agent</term> <note>original Latin: *Vivum agens*; the active, vital principle that directs the transformation</note> or the ruling spirit of the soul and the body. For this <term>Ether</term> is its seat and <term>refreshment</term> <note>original Latin: *Refrigerium*; a place of cool repose or cooling relief</note>, in which it rests as if in its point and <term>center</term>, and rules at the same time, directing all <term>degrees of fire</term> <note>original Latin: *Gradus ignis*</note> itself. But if it is once thrown from its throne and out of its heaven, *by a degree of fire stronger than is proper* <note>original Latin: *per gradum ignis justo fortiorem*</note>, it will never come back in again, because its <term>center</term> and soul are killed, and thus it cannot rise, rule, nor govern, but instead hides itself in the body, which it compresses into a red and useless hard stone before the proper time—a stone which has no life and provides no benefit, because it [has lost] its <term>living agent</term> and <term>soul</term> <note>original Latin: *Animam*</note>.

# EXPLANATION

...has lost; therefore, guard yourself only against strong fire, otherwise you have no *difficulty* to fear, for the *Philosopher* <note>original: *Philosophus*; likely referring to a specific authority like Hermes Trismegistus or Geber</note> intends this alone: that you should not rush, but rather follow Nature. *For haste in this natural art brings the highest and greatest harm* <note>original Latin: *nam festinatio in hac arte naturali summum dat nocumentum*</note>: Haste brings the highest and greatest damage. *However, the slowest cooking or digestion brings the highest benefit* <note>original Latin: *Lentissima autem decoctio vel digestio summum adfert emolumentum*</note>: A mild, slow, and gentle fire brings forth timely and heavenly fruits. *Therefore, in this art, always keep in mind: make haste slowly* <note>original Latin: *festina lente*</note>.

7. *On the other side of the characters* there appears a *concise philosophical machine for completing the physical work*, containing a phial and lamp-light. This *machine*, which represents a complete *sophic laboratory* <note>original: *Laboratorium sophicum*; "sophic" refers to the true, wise practice of alchemy as opposed to "vulgar" chemistry</note>, is, with its secret *operation*, far removed from other *laboratories, common operations, <term>athanors</term>* <note>a furnace designed to provide steady, moderate heat for long periods, often called a "palingenesis furnace"</note>, blast-, calcining-, and fixing-furnaces. This is because in this natural *operation*, no trace of these violent and <term>sophistic</term> <note>in this context, "sophistic" means "false" or "deceptive" methods that force nature rather than following it</note> *labors* is to be found. Instead, just as Nature *acts* in the mountains, and *operates* in the closed *cavities* and caves of the mountains, and *matures* in its *due matrices* <note>original: *Matricibus debitis*; the "wombs" or surrounding rock where ores grow</note>, and *produces* the most precious stones and *metals*, so it is also here in this naturally formed *machine*, whose outer shape and *body* is like a closed...

...is viewed as a mountain. The inner **metallic egg** <note>original Latin: *ovum metallicum*</note> is the cavity and hollow of the mountain, within which the **wondrous generations of nature** <note>original Latin: *generationes mirabiles naturae*</note> take place. The glass, however, and the **physical egg** <note>original Latin: *ovum physicum*</note> is the **matrix** <note>the womb or formative environment</note>, into which one places the seed or pure spirit only once, and seals it **hermetically**—that is, **most exquisitely** <note>original Latin: *exquisitissime*</note>—fusing it with finely crushed glass and pure borax. It is then placed into the cavity of this artificial mountain or machine **until perfect maturation and the solid constitution of our Androgyne** <note>original Latin: *usque ad maturationem perfectam, & solidam Androgyni nostri constitutionem*. The "Androgyne" is a famous alchemical symbol for the Philosopher's Stone, representing the perfect union of masculine (sulfur/sun) and feminine (mercury/moon) principles.</note>. This process is no different than how a child thrives toward maturity in the mother’s womb through gentle warmth and carefulness; after ten months, it breaks the mother and is born into the light of the world. So it is also with our **physical Androgyne**, which is kept in its matrix with gentle warmth and brought to maturity, and after ten months appears and is born in the physical egg as a **King of Glory**, possessing all natural qualities and **supernatural virtues** <note>original Latin: *virtutibus supernaturalibus*</note>.

The light placed beneath represents the warmth of the natural **fire** <note>the original uses the symbol $\Delta$ for fire</note>, which must awaken the secret and internal **fire** <note>symbol: $\Delta$</note> of the **physical body** <note>original Latin: *corporis physici*</note>, so that it does not burn its pure body, but rather must direct, mature, and fix it **toward the highest perfection** <note>original Latin: *ad summam perfectionem*</note>.

<header>EXPLANATION</header>

...unworthy ones away from this noble paradise. This is to say: if the **Philosopher** <note>original: *Philosophus*; the alchemist</note> **operates** <note>original: *operiret*</note> according to his own will and not God’s will to benefit God and the poor, then he shall not be permitted to see the **physical rainbow** <note>original: *Iridem physicum*</note>, known as the philosophical **sky of colors** <note>original: *Farben-Himmel*; in alchemy, this multicolored stage is often called the "Peacock's Tail," signaling the matter is purifying</note>. For this **rainbow** <note>original: *Iris*</note> is God's **sign of grace** <note>original: *Gnaden-Zeichen*</note> regarding one’s worthiness or unworthiness; it consists of high and shining metallic colors which no one but a true **Philosopher** has seen, **on account of the marvelousness of its supreme beauty and splendor** <note>original: *propter mirabilitatem summæ pulchritudinis & nitoris*</note>. All other colors, however beautiful they may appear, are merely "water-colors" and can be **produced** <note>original: *produciret*</note> in all **subjects** <note>original: *Subjectis*; substances</note>, but they receive no **consideration** <note>original: *Consideration*</note> from the **Sages** <note>original: *Sophis*</note>.

11. Now follows the great and rich **Miraculous Tree** <note>original: *Wunder-Baum*</note> with its red philosophical fruits. This is the true Tree of Life, of riches, of wisdom, of the highest health and long life—the tree of all pleasure, all joy, and the delights of this world. Whoever enjoys this divine fruit and applies it to the benefit of their neighbor is the most blessed person on earth; through their wisdom, they see above all the wise men of the world and fathom nature with its **mysteries** <note>original: *Mysteriis*</note> and **wonders** <note>original: *Mirabilibus*</note> to the deepest degree. For such a person has become a god, a master of nature in its **internal operations and actions** <note>original: *Operationibus und Actionibus internis*</note>, through which he can **perform miracles** <note>original: *Miracula præstiren*</note> and **preserve** <note>original: *conserviren*</note> himself **against the fates of the world** <note>original: *contra mundi fata*</note>.

12. Now comes the **red lion** in our secret work and philosophical table; among the **Sages** <note>original: *Sophis*; referring to the true alchemists or "wise ones"</note>, this represents the highest **fixity** <note>the state of being stable and resistant to change by fire</note> of our **Wonder-Tincture**. For when this has arisen through its orderly **governance** <note>original: *Regiment*; the controlled application of heat over time</note>, it performs **miracles** <note>original: *Miracula*</note> in its **operation**, and swallows the metallic **Gold** <term>☉</term> <note>the astrological and alchemical symbol for the Sun, representing gold</note> **during its projection upon it** <note>original: *in projectione super illud*; "projection" refers to the final step of the Great Work where the Stone is cast onto molten metal to transform it</note>, so that no one notices where it has gone. This is because the lion transforms the gold into its own nature and highest tincture, as a **ferment**. Thus, this mighty and strong lion tears apart all metals and brings them into its own property; it overcomes the strongest thing in the world, and in this constant victory and struggle, it finally dies. But its death gives life to all things in the world, because through this death it rises again gloriously anew, and increases its powers and virtues manifold and unspeakably. This lion is born **immediately** <note>original: *immediate*</note> after our fiery **Salamander** <note>a symbol of the matter that can survive and thrive in the fire</note> in the **physical egg** <note>original: *in ovo physico*; the sealed glass vessel, or "Philosophical Egg," where the transformation occurs</note>. Regarding this, the Sages say: **"make a Salamander out of the Phoenix, that is: make our spirit standing in the physical egg and persevering in the fire; thus you shall obtain our struggling lion, overcoming all things in this world"** <note>original: *fac Salamandram ex Phœnice, hoc est, fac spiritum nostrum in ovo physico stantem, & in igne perseverantem, sic obtinebis leonem nostrum certantem, in hoc mundo omnia superantem*</note>. This **analogy** <note>original: *Analogicum*</note> is **explained** <note>original: *expliciret*</note> by our red lion. Now, whoever has the good fortune to obtain and rightly use this **monster of the wise** <note>original: *Monstrum sapientum*; here "monster" means a divine or supernatural marvel</note> in the **great physical work** <note>original: *magno opere physico*; the quest for the Philosopher's Stone</note>, he has

...neither blossom nor fruit, but remains eternally unfruitful. Its trunk is compared to the **knowledge of all sophistry**; its large and wide-extending branches, however, are the wondrous heads of the sophists who wish to produce nothing but miracles, great deeds, and wonders in the <term>sophistical art</term> <note>original: *arte sophistica*; here referring to false alchemy based on trickery or misunderstanding of nature</note>. The many great branches of the Tree of Sophistry represent the countless false processes through which many have come to misfortune and fallen into poverty. Its innumerable sprouts on the branches represent the recipes of sophistry where, at last, after all applied effort, labor, and expense, the <term>operator</term> <note>the practitioner performing the alchemical experiment</note> receives a "deception" <note>original: *decipe*; the author is making a pun on the word *recipe*, suggesting that the "recipe" for false gold is actually a "deception"</note> instead of a recipe, and must depart empty-handed. This is the fruit of sophistry, which we wish to note in passing <note>original: *in parenthesi*</note>, so that one may guard against it with the utmost diligence.

This aforementioned <term>Union of the Sun and Moon</term> <note>original: *Conjunctio Solis und Lunæ*; the symbolic marriage of the male/solar and female/lunar principles</note>, which no one but the wise can see, occurs in the <term>great physical work</term> <note>original: *magno opere physico*; the ultimate alchemical process</note> three times in a single year of operation. Because this <term>white viscous spirit</term> <note>original: *Spiritus viscosus albus*</note> is compared to the moist **Moon** <note>original: *Lunæ*</note>, and the <term>dry red soul</term> <note>original: *anima rubicunda sicca*</note> to the **Sun**, this <term>conjunction of seeds</term> <note>original: *Conjunctio seminum*</note> is held by the <term>Sages</term> <note>original: *Sophis*; in this context, the author refers to the true masters of wisdom, contrasting with the "sophists" or frauds mentioned on the previous page</note> to be the first and most secret meeting of the **Sun** and **Moon**.

The second **conjunction** of these most noble **stars** <note>original: *Astrorum*; referring to the alchemical Sun and Moon</note> occurs <term>in the middle of the physical work</term> <note>original: *in medio operis physici*</note>, when the white queen enters into the **Star of Mars** <note>original: *Astrum Martis*</note>, and the red **Venus** presents herself with her color. At this point, the **Sun** and **Moon** come together for the second time, and they are fixed or united eternally.

For the third time, this high **conjunction** arises when the white, fixed queen (or tincture) is <term>immediately joined and coupled</term> <note>original: *immediate... conjungiret und copuliret*</note> with the red and fixed king in the **physical egg**. There, in three days, a true birth arises, and the white queen goes <term>entirely</term> <note>original: *in totum*</note> into the red king, and is completely <term>absorbed</term> by him out of heartfelt love and kinship. From this **conjunction**, the king receives his highest **quality**, and thereby his virtue is <term>exalted to infinity</term> <note>original: *in infinitum exaltiret*</note>, so that he <term>performs miracles in all diseases</term> <note>original: *in morbis omnibus miracula præstiret*</note>,

...must be driven off, so that no corruption arises in the **physical heaven** <note>original: *coelo physico*; the upper, "sky-like" portion of the alchemical vessel where vapors circulate</note>, which these **Stymphalian birds** <note>original: *aves stymphalides*; a reference to the man-eating birds of Greek mythology, used here to represent dangerous or corrosive volatile vapors</note> and **Mercurial birds** <note>original: *Mercuriales*</note> intend with all their might through their flight and migration. For upon this single **act** <note>original: *Actu*</note> and manual skill of taming these birds hangs the entire art with all its knowledge and subtlety; everything else compared to this **act** is **child’s play** <note>original: *ludus puerorum*</note> and **women’s work** <note>original: *opus mulierum*; a traditional alchemical phrase referring to the later stages of the work which require only patient, gentle heat</note>, because in everything one must guide oneself by the **philosophical ether**, as if by a compass, if one does not wish to go astray on this Sea of the Sages and wreck or ruin one’s ship or **physical egg** <note>original: *ovum physicum*; the sealed glass flask used in the Great Work</note> upon the rocks of ignorance and the mountains of fire. This compass and **physical heaven** is a small **space** <note>original: *Spatium*</note> **under the hermetic seal of our physical egg** <note>original: *sub sigillo hermetico ovi nostri physici*</note>, <margin>page 219</margin> which **space** is three fingers wide,

->### PHILOSOPHICAL TABLES <note>original: *TABULÆ PHILOSOPHICÆ*</note><-

...is, and must not be filled with heat, nor should such heat be driven into it; otherwise, a **corruption** of the **body** and the **soul** will immediately arise, and from the **physical heaven** <note>original: *ex cœlo physico*; the upper part of the vessel where vapors collect</note> a **physical abyss** <note>original: *Barathrum physicum*</note> and a **hell** will emerge. For hell is nothing other than a corruption in which all impurities and extremes are violently introduced. In such a case, the **work** <note>original: *Werck*; the alchemical process toward the Philosopher's Stone</note> would be spoiled. Therefore, all **sons of art** <note>original: *filii artis*; a traditional term for initiates into alchemical secrets</note> should guard themselves in all **operations** <note>original: *Operationibus*</note> against even the slightest violence or haste in this **natural art**. For nature suffers no violence; one must only come to her aid with gentle warmth, as she herself is the wisest mistress in her operation, whom we must follow in all things. This should be well noted, for it is the **essential factor** <note>original: *Fac totum*; literally "do everything," or the most vital point</note>.

The remaining **symbols** <note>original: *Characteres*</note> and their sections aim solely and exclusively to represent the **seven separations** <note>original: *7. Separationes*; the stages of refining and dividing the substance</note> that occur in this **philosophical operation**. Since these are all perfectly described in their proper order within this **little treatise** <note>original: *Tractätlein*</note>, and one truth does not differ from another, I have only wished to show the most noble and perfect operation within it. For that which is of great importance, and upon which the whole **work** and **purpose** <note>original: *Scopus*</note> depends, must be most deeply impressed upon the **artist** <note>original: *Artisten*; the alchemist</note>, so that he does not take an **indirect path** <note>original: *Viam indirectam*</note>, but instead travels the **royal road** <note>original: *Viam regiam*</note> and achieves a successful conclusion.

16. The **Symbols of Solomon** <note>original: *Characteres Salomonis*</note> are somewhat more profound and thought-provoking in a magical manner, which one sees right from the beginning <note>original: *ab initio*</note> in the **Sun** and **Moon** <note>original: *Sol und Luna*; representing Gold and Silver or the active and passive principles</note>. The Sun and Moon represent the purest <term>Sulfur<note>original symbol: 🜍</note></term> and <term>Mercury<note>original symbol: ☿</note></term>. The Subject placed between two sieves <note>original: *Sieben*; a pun in German which can mean "sevens" or "sieves"</note> represents the very first <term>sifting<note>original: *Cribration*</note></term> in the <term>physical egg<note>original: *ovo physico*; the hermetic glass vessel</note></term>, or the dissolution of the **Sulfur** and **Mercury**. For the philosophers have their <term>philosophical sieves<note>original: *Cribra sophica*</note></term> and instruments within the **physical egg**, and they sift or purify their subjects without laying a hand on them, **with fire and internal Azoth until the perfection of the operation** <note>original: *cum igne & Azoth interne usque ad perfectionem operationis*</note>. The flowers or tulips signify the wondrous beauty of this <term>physical subject<note>original: *Subjecti physici*</note></term> or <term>Virgin Earth<note>original: *Terræ virgineæ*</note></term>.

☉ The two doves signify the union of the two seeds, male and female, as the first secret meeting of the **Sun** and **Moon** mentioned above; this is the first crown that the <term>Artist<note>original: *Artista*; the alchemist</note></term> conquers. ### PHILOSOPHICAL TABLES.

...of the spirit. The four-sided sign with the zero in the **middle** <note>original: *medio*</note> signifies the **Soul** <note>original: *Anima*</note> of this spirit, at which two hands on both sides point and indicate that this **Soul**, as the **living agent** <note>original: *Vivum agens*</note>, must be observed above all else, if the **Subject** <note>original: *Subjectum*</note> and **Mercury** <note>original: *Mercurius*</note> are not to perish **entirely** <note>original: *in totum*</note>. When the **Subject of spirit and soul** <note>original: *Subjectum spiritus & animæ*</note> is thus **sifted** <note>original: *cribrire't*</note>, exalted, and purified within the **physical egg** <note>original: *in ovo physico*; the sealed glass vessel</note> in a **philosophical manner** <note>original: *philosophico modo*</note> in all purity, and brought **to the consistency** <note>original: *ad consistentiam*</note> of white <term>Antimony<note>original symbol: ♁; here referring to the purified, white earth or the 'Star Regulus'</note></term>, then you have received two crowns in this philosophical triumph, which is the true interpretation of the first part of the **Table of Solomon**.

In the second part, our **Mercury** <note>original: *Mercurius*</note> immediately appears first; it proceeds with its white **Sulfur** <note>original: *Sulphur*</note> and transfigured body through its purity **toward the perfection of the fixed moon** <note>original: *ad perfectionem lunæ fixæ*; representing the creation of Silver</note>, an operation specifically indicated by the **Pentagon** or the five-sided figure. Then the **Artist** <note>the alchemist</note> receives and holds the key to all of nature, which the **sign** <note>original: *Signum*</note> following the **Moon** <note>original symbol: ☽</note> indicates. Then he proceeds in the operation, penetrating ever further into the treasury of nature, and attains the lemon-red <term>Fire<note>original symbol: △</note></term> and the **Fire of Venus** <note>original: *Venus-△*</note>, which stands marked with three points, finally bringing it to the complete and perfect **Yellowness of the Sages** <note>original: *Gilbe der Weisen*; the *citrinitas* stage of the process</note>, as a double **mercurial Fire** <note>original: *☿alisches △*</note>, which is drawn thus: <image-desc>alchemical symbol: a triangle with the mercury symbol ☿ positioned directly on its top vertex</image-desc>. This is then brought to the perfect redness and color **of the Sun** <note>original: *Solis*; the *rubedo* stage representing Gold</note>, which redness indicates the key to the entire **Mastery** <note>original: *Meisterschafft*</note>.

### EXPLANATION OF THE PHILOSOPHICAL TABLES.

Subsequently, this fixed red **Mercury** <term>Mercury<note>Symbol: ☿</note></term> <note>original: *rubicundus fixus*</note> passes through the **physical sieve** <note>original: *Cribrum physicum*; a metaphor for a refining process</note> into **Exaltation** <note>the process of refining and increasing the potency of a substance</note>, and is formed anew, and constitutes a triple fixed **Mercury** <term>Mercury<note>Symbol: ☿</note></term>, or a triple fixed heavenly **Salt**; then it returns to its fiery redness and eternal consistency and permanence in the fire, as our **Salamander** <note>original: *Salimander*; a mythical creature believed to live in fire, representing a substance that resists heat without being consumed</note> that endures in the fire; afterward it takes on the highest redness and **fixation** <note>the process of making a volatile substance stable so it no longer evaporates when heated</note> of the **Lion**, which all the **Signs** <note>original: *Signa*</note> show in due order, and arrives at a double **Perfection**, which is indicated and represented by the double **Pentagon**; then the **Artist** and **Sage** <note>original: *Sophus*</note> have won 3 **crowns**.

The first, namely, through the art and science of the union of the two **seeds** <note>original: *Saamen*</note>, male and female in nature, without which subtle master-science no one can bring the **physical offspring** <note>original: *Fœtum physicum*</note> or **Tincture** <note>the potent elixir or Philosopher's Stone</note> to light; for where there is no union of these two seeds, nothing can be born but mere **monstrosities** <note>original: *Monstra*</note>, which are called **aborted fetuses** <note>original: *Fœtus abortivos*</note> and serve no purpose, but rather cause harm.

The second and double **crown** <note>original: *Crone*</note> he wins with the white **Mercury** <term>Mercury<note>Symbol: ☿</note></term> of the Sages, as their first **Medicine** and perfect white **Tincture**, which can make him blessed in this earthly life and in all the aforementioned matters, and obtain everyone’s favor, and effect health in them.

But the triple **crown** the Artist wins in the **Exaltation** and **Multiplication** <note>the process of increasing the quantity and power of the Stone</note> of this philosophical work, as a double and supreme **Perfection**, with which he performs wonders and works great things in all of nature, as can be seen at length in this **little treatise** <note>original: *Tractätlein*</note>.

## OF UNIVERSAL CREATION.

The exploration of Creation, through which God has revealed Himself with all His wonders, is the greatest wisdom and is the foundation of all high sciences <term>Wissenschafften</term>. It also places the most useful application of these sciences directly into the hands of the "Sons of Wisdom" <note>A traditional term in alchemy and Christian mysticism referring to those who seek a deeper, spiritual understanding of nature and the divine.</note>; for from this, one can perfectly learn and recognize not only the true knowledge of God, Nature, and Man, but also the sequence <note>original: "Suite"</note> of other sciences, which are linked together like a chain, where one necessary conclusion locks into the next as links in a sequence. Therefore, no one can deny that all creatures receive their powers from the eternal origin, from which the preservation and propagation of all things also stem.

When one thus returns to the origin of all things, one soon becomes aware of a beginning according to the literal <note>original: "selbstlautenden," meaning self-sounding or literal.</note> value of Holy Scripture. This beginning is called by the philosophers the **created mystery** <term>Mysterium creatum <gloss>the initial state of potentiality in creation</gloss></term>, from whose **center** <term>Centro</term> the **Chaos** <term>Chaos</term> [emerges] according to the...

...will of the Creator, out of which the separation had to occur; from this, the **binary number** <note>original: "Numerus binarius"; referring to the duality of two opposing forces.</note> arose—namely, **Light** and **Darkness**. These both remained in a prefiguration through the connection of the eternal original **Principle** <note>original: "Principii"</note>; hence the inclination of one toward the other arose, since such [creation] arose individually and not from diverse materials. Thus, in the **mixtures** <note>original: "Mixtis"; referring to compound substances or physical bodies composed of multiple elements.</note>, something always remained that cannot manifest <note>original: "exseriren"</note> itself on its own, even though it contains much Light and Darkness. Accordingly, even the heavens—the actual **receptacle** <note>original: "Receptaculum"</note> of Light—still retained something of Darkness, because even the heavens are not pure compared to the unspeakable light of God. The Earth, however, and the Abyss—as the house of Darkness—were not robbed of all light, by means of which they might reach **exaltation, renovation, and melioration** <note>original: "Exaltation, Renovation und Melioration"; these are alchemical and theosophical terms for the process of refining, renewing, and improving a substance or soul.</note>.

For that same divine radiated light always remains in its bodies, yet in a completely different way in the heavens than in earthly things; for in the latter, it manifests itself quite darkly, but in the heavens, it expresses itself much more brightly, powerfully, and capably for the propagation of all creatures. Whatever God has brought forth, both in heaven and on earth, He also preserves and directs according to His will;

### Regarding the Highest Medicine.

Indeed, heavenly things are all guided more closely by **God's** light; but here below, whatever is found in animals, plants, minerals, and other creatures must take its direction by means of the intervention of the heavens. Within this process are actually contained the <term>first principles</term> <note>original Latin: "prima principia"; the fundamental starting points or primary causes of existence.</note> of their continuation and preservation, as a result of which the form and shape of all good things are found under the form of heaven or light.

Thus, there is a single power and ability through which everything else is animated and made capable. For this reason, heaven and light can produce much without seed, but the seed can bring forth nothing without the aid of heaven. Rather, in the seed, there is only a good <term>preparation</term> <note>original: "Præparation"</note> and conditioning of the matter to await something greater; then, heaven breathes the light of life into such a prepared <term>subject</term> <note>original: "Subjectum"; here referring to the physical base or recipient of a spiritual influence.</note> by means of the light-spirit, which is, as it were, the driver or <term>vehicle</term> <note>original: "Vehiculum"; a medium that carries a spiritual force into the material world.</term> that brings heaven and earth together. Therefore, this light-spirit or <term>spirit of the universe</term> <note>original Latin: "Spiritus universi"; the vital, pervasive soul of the world in alchemical philosophy.</note> spreads everywhere and shows itself to be fertile throughout; for this spirit holds everything within itself, it warms and awakens everything, and its power and ability even penetrate into the soul.

# True and Unerring Way

This power coming from heaven does not extend further than the <term>capacity</term> <note>original: "Capacitas"</note> of the <term>subject</term> <note>original: "Subjecti"; in this context, the physical body or material base that receives a spiritual influence.</note> in which it encloses itself. For this <term>essential power</term> <note>original: "Tugend-Krafft"; here "virtue" (Tugend) refers to an inherent, active power or medicinal property within a substance.</note> is not uniform in all things, nor does it *proceed* in the same manner; rather, it follows the position, course, and nature of the stars. Since these stars show frequent changes, the effect of the power also appears different, even though the <term>spirit of the universe</term> <note>original: "Spiritus universi"</note> remains one and the same and unchanging.

If this spirit were able to act on its own without any other influence, we would experience nothing but uniform <term>manifestations</term> <note>original: "Productiones"</note>; for it contains nothing but the purest essence of life. However, it cannot bestow more than the <term>matter</term> <note>original: "Materia"</note> destined to receive it is capable of receiving, or as is made possible through the intervention of the stars.

When a dark body approaches the light, its darkness is immediately seized by the light and connected to heaven through this illumination, so that it may receive its virtue and spiritual essence all the more eagerly and *adequately*. Anyone who wishes to reflect upon this will easily be able to observe the connection between all creatures; the inclination of one toward another must clearly show itself—

turns away from **God**; and because the aforementioned light is the fountainhead of all powers, a person who lives so far separated from it will be able to achieve all the less.

The senses have the light of the soul <note>original: "Gemüthes"; refers to the inner spiritual mind, encompassing both intellect and emotion.</note> enclosed within their center. However, the man who can bring the sensory perceptions from the wide-ranging outer circle to the center—and conversely, who is able to expand that which is from the midpoint—is a true <term>Cabalist</term> <note>In the 17th-century context, a "Cabalist" was a scholar of Christian Kabbalah who sought hidden divine truths through meditation and scripture.</note> and a scholar of **God**. They have been illuminated by the bright flame of love of the divine Spirit; these are the true servants of **God**, who even in this life may notice a degree of <term>transfiguration</term> <note>A spiritual transformation where the physical body begins to reflect divine light.</note> within themselves, following the example of **Enoch** and **Elijah** <note>Biblical prophets who were famously "translated" or taken up to heaven without dying a natural death.</note>.

As to how one can elevate his spirit to such a degree that he may enter into conversation with **God**, look to the Holy Scriptures; for they will show that the way has been sufficiently demonstrated by our Savior, **Jesus**. Therefore, one should simply hold to the teachings of **Christ**, and thus one will grasp **God** in the light, and by Him be enlight-

# True and Unerring Path

The third, namely the soul, which was from eternity within the light-spirit of **God**, but was made manifest through the eternal Word and the fiery spirit of love. This fiery spirit of love remains infinite within **God** and is the ruling spirit of the **Lord**, by which—as He spoke through the eternal Word—all creatures and their hosts came into being; and within that eternal Word was the very same fiery spirit that hovered over the waters in **God's** creation.

This wisdom of **God** the Father, as the eternal light-spirit, and the eternal Word, have accomplished everything within themselves in an incomprehensible and invisible manner. However, so that these mysteries resting within **God** might be revealed, the fiery spirit of **God's** love descended from the eternal Word and manifested itself corporeally <note>original: "Cörperlich"; meaning taking on a physical or material form.</note>, as He willed that the fire of love and the eternal spiritual light should go forth into and out of itself, within the human body of the virgin <note>original: "Jungfrauen"; likely referring to the Virgin Mary in the context of the Incarnation, or the "Sophic Virgin" in alchemical mysticism representing pure, uncorrupted matter.</note>, and thus what was hidden became manifest. Furthermore, the supernatural fire within the eternal Word was to be glorified through natural rotation <term>Rotation: in alchemical philosophy, this refers to the *circulatio*, the repeating cycles of distillation or spiritual refinement intended to "glorify" or perfect a substance.</term>; for when the fiery spirit of **God** moved over the water, the division among all creatures occurred through the eternal Word, and these creatures became partakers of the eternal divine fire.

For this **Water** <term>$\nabla$</term> cannot exist without **Salt** <term>$\ominus$: Salt</term>, for the salt contains within itself the **Fire** <term>$\triangle$</term> and **Air** through the movement of the Spirit of the Creator.

**True and Unerring Way**

## PART II.
### CHAPTER I.
# CONCERNING ALCHEMY OR THE SPAGYRIC ART. <note>original Latin: *DE ALCHYMIA SEU ARTE SPAGYRICA*</note>

**Alchemy** is a **practice of philosophy** <note>original: *Practica der Philosophie*</note> and a **wonder-work of nature**, whereby the most noble things are joined together through **art** and the **working of nature**, and are transformed into something better through their own mixing. The **form** <note>original: *Gestalt*</note> of any thing, however, may not be changed unless it is first brought into a **spiritual essence**. This is achieved through the assistance of art—namely, through the **diligence of the artist** and of the **fire**, which is governed slowly and carefully according to nature throughout the entire work. For by this means, the **Quintessence** <term>Quintessence: From the Latin *quinta essentia* (fifth essence). Alchemists believed that beyond the four elements of earth, air, fire, and water, there was a fifth, purest element that contained the life-force of a substance.</term> is drawn out from a spiritual essence through the **separation of the elements** and their **reunion**, specifically through **soul** and **spirit**.

Therefore, this art is a **mirror of the secret wisdom of God**, because within it the entire nature of all things in the whole world is seen as if in a clear mirror. For this reason, nature must be sought not in the external essence, but in her **inner hiddenness**, and must be viewed with [one's own] eyes, because the art is a...

<summary>This page defines alchemy as a philosophical practice and a divine mirror that allows the practitioner to see the hidden inner nature of all things. It explains the core process of refining matter into its spiritual quintessence by using controlled fire to separate and reunite soul and spirit.</summary>

**follower of nature**; for there is no art where nature is not there first, for art is improved by nature, through natural movement and **action** <note>original: *Agirung*</note>, whereby subtle nature discovers the **form** which is enclosed in every thing; and where nature ceases to work, there **art** first begins, and **perfects** <note>original: *perficiret*</note> nature. Therefore, the **Artist** <note>In this context, the "Artist" is the practicing alchemist.</note> is an **imitator of nature**, and must know and be capable of [understanding] the nature of all things, so that he does not exceed her, but follows her in all **limits** <note>original Latin: *Limitibus*</note>, and must well understand the causes of all things, their **seeds**, **principles**, **elements** <note>original Latin: *Semina, Principia, Elementa*—the fundamental building blocks of matter in alchemical theory</note>, how they have arisen and been **generated** <note>original: *generiret*</note>, and what they are capable of doing and suffering in nature.

Therefore, this divine art is kept in the **power of God**, who gives it to whom He wills. Thus, it does not depend on human power, nor was it thought out or invented by them: therefore, keep the **fear of God** before your eyes, and do not despair, you **son of wisdom** <note>A traditional term for a student or initiate of the alchemical mysteries.</note>, and through manifold contemplation you will finally arrive where your mind desires; for because this divine science is noble, it is also deeply hidden, and this occurs as an **obscuring** <note>original: *Verdunckelung*</note> to the ungodly, but for the pious, the way is thereby prepared, so that in seeking without ceasing they pray to God for such art, and that is the foremost cause and

<meta>This page continues the discussion on the spiritual and practical requirements of alchemy. ### True and Infallible Way

...difficulty in this art; for it is possible for no human, except through **God's inspiration** and **enlightenment**, or through the means of the **revelation** <note>original: *Offenbarung*</note> of a faithful friend, to attain such an art.

But he who does not lay a hand to it himself, and does not apply his head and senses to it, and is lazy in the reading of the **philosophical writings** <note>The "philosophers" here are the alchemists of old; their "writings" are the cryptic texts that guide the practitioner.</note>, he will also be found [lacking] in the preparation of our **matter** <term>Matter (materia): The raw, physical substance used at the beginning of the alchemical process.</term>, and in the natural **operation** and **maturation** <note>The process of "ripening" or aging the chemical substance until it reaches perfection.</note>; for because one book explains the other, and what is missing in one is found in the other, it is impossible to find everything belonging to this art orderly in books, but rather through **God's ray of grace**; from this source comes this precious treasure down from above, from the **Father of Light**, and not from below, out of **mines** <note>original: *Bergwercken*</note> and books. Since this art is divine, it is impossible for men to understand it, neither through reading nor studying, even if they were the wisest and most learned in the world, if God does not initially reveal it to them; for in this place all natural **reason** <note>original: *Vernunfft*</note> and cause are lacking, which is why God has reserved it only for His own, and it does not lie within human power to attain **perfection** therein.

### Toward the Highest Medicine.

<meta>Catchword: So</meta>

If you govern the **Work** <note>original: *Werck*</note> in this way, you shall obtain the perfect Art with all good works, and the entire philosophical mastery <note>original: *philosophische Meisterschafft*; the complete realization of the alchemist's goal</note>; but take very good care that you do not overstep the goal in this precious work, then you will be blessed and most happy, for one thing you should note above all others:

You must not hasten in our work, as some ignorant sophists <note>original: *Sophisten*; a term used for those who practice alchemy incorrectly or without spiritual insight</note> do, who wish for the Son of the Sun <note>original: *Sohn der Sonnen*; a name for the philosopher's stone or the perfected gold</note> to be born in one year or nine months, like a grain <margin><unclear>and ... not?</unclear></margin> or a child in the mother’s womb; oh no, for the Sun <note>original: *Sol*</note> and the Moon <note>original: *Luna*</note> do not grow in one year or nine months like the fragile human and the grain, for the more noble a thing is by nature, and the more perfectly it is generated, the more time it requires for its growth; for that which arises quickly also passes away quickly; grain and man are born quickly, and therefore their life is also short; but the Sun and the Moon are not to be considered in this way; rather, they are a lasting and fiery salamander <note>a mythical lizard believed to live in fire; here representing a substance that is "fixed" and cannot be consumed by heat</note> that lives in the fire, is indestructible, and proves itself incorruptible <note>original: *incorruptibel*; meaning it cannot decay or be destroyed</note>. This mineral and heavenly spirit is, by its very essence, of a much more perfect nature than fleshly man; therefore it also gives him long life and protects mankind from all frailty and days of sickness.

## ON PUTREFACTION.
<note>original: DE PUTREFACTIONE; the alchemical process of decay or fermentation, which practitioners believed was necessary to "kill" the raw material so its spirit could be released.</note>

Everything that has been created, and is still born daily, must perish and rot, so that it may rejoice in its new birth; for everything that is born and created has no rest, because Nature always works to multiply its own kind, for which it was ordained by God. Therefore, **putrefaction** is the first operation in this art, and it reveals the very greatest secret, because it is the true separation and transformation of the elements; for the <term>Soul</term> <note>original: *Anima*; the vital essence or life-force of the substance</note> is thereby drawn out, and the spirit is made penetrating, so that it gives a new life to its body, the fruits of which are better than all the world's wealth.

This <term>Putrefaction</term> <note>decay or decomposition</note> is now a change of the first form in our <term>Chaos</term> <note>the raw, unrefined starting material of the alchemist's work</note>, from which fruit and multiplication follow through refinement, as it is within this that the germinal natures show themselves to be alive. There, the spirits and their "blossoming" begin to work most sharply until the end of perfection, which occurs through the convenient warmth of the external fire, without ceasing to digest and cook. Then Nature begins to multiply and strengthen herself, and also to ripen her fruits; and here the art is hidden most secretly of all by the wise; for one must [treat] this philosophical body

# The True and Unerring Way

Therefore, if you work wisely with all diligence and do not exceed nature's goal with haste, you will encounter wonder and great joy; and blessed are you who understands this in an orderly fashion. For this reason, cook and grind our <term>Sulfur 🜍</term> <note>original: *Sulfur*; in alchemy, Sulfur represents the active, masculine principle and the "soul" of the matter, not just the common chemical element.</note> with fire and not with your hands. Begin the process again and do not let it vex you to start over often, even though the entire Work takes a long time; for it is accomplished through nothing other than tedious cooking. Then you shall obtain the great secret of the Sages and, through this gift, be called a spiritual son of the highest light, who surpasses all the wise of this world with his teaching, and whose clarity and the virtuous power of his <term>Medicine ℞</term> <note>original: *Recipe/Medicine*; the symbol for the completed Tincture or Philosopher's Stone.</note> exceeds all the treasures of this world.

Our operation in this high Work is conducted according to the four seasons of the year. In the first labor of our laudable Work, there appears <margin>and dissolution</margin> the damp and rainy winter, and that is <term>Putrefaction</term> <note>The process of "rotting" or breaking down the substance until it turns black (the *Nigredo* stage).</note>, where everything decays and is corrupted. In the second, the warm and blooming spring, which is the <term>Decoction</term> <note>Boiling or heating.</note>, <term>Activation</term> <note>original: *Agirung*; from the Latin *agere*, meaning to set in motion or act upon.</note>, and rising. In the third, the hot and dry summer, which is the <term>Dealbation</term> <note>The "whitening" or *Albedo* stage, where the matter is purified.</note> and <term>Calcination</term> <note>Reduction to a white powder through heat.</note>. In the fourth, the rich, fruit-bearing autumn, which is the <term>Rubefaction</term> <note>The "reddening" or *Rubedo* stage, signaling the final perfection of the Stone.</note> and <term>Fixation</term> <note>Making the volatile substance stable and permanent under heat.</note>. # Regarding the Highest Medicine.

He who does not have the **Spirit** <note>original: *Spiritum*</note> from its own body and from its own property cannot take it from a foreign body and operate with it effectively; for all those who err by working outside of natural things are deceived, and are deceivers themselves.

For this reason, the work must be conducted according to nature and not otherwise, because the nature of a thing is only improved within its own essence. This high art is called divine because all natural causes cease there—specifically during the transformation of colors and the new birth. It is at this stage that the greatest error of this art occurs: either the **decoction** <term>Decoction (original: *Decoction*; the process of refining a substance through steady, controlled heating)</term> happens too quickly, or too much fire is applied. For our **mineral water** <note>original symbol: ▽</note> and **Azoth** <note>A term for the essential transformative agent or "universal solvent" sought by alchemists.</note> are slowly hardened and matured through the action and effect of their own **sulfur** <note>original: *Sulphuris*; in alchemy, sulfur represents the internal solar fire or soul of the matter.</note>. Through long and constant gentle cooking, they gain the power to transform all metals to which they are added into the finest **gold** <note>original symbol: ☉</note>.

Here, understanding of the art is essential. Therefore, let all other things go and pay attention only to the completion of this natural work; for the art is rooted in nature and yet surpasses nature, because nature cannot complete this task without the artist. # The True and Infallible Path

<margin>The alchemical symbol for Mercury <image-desc>A circle with a cross below and a crescent/horns on top</image-desc></margin>
But the **Azoth** <term>Azoth: the "mercury of the philosophers," considered the essential agent of transformation and the universal solvent</term> or the golden **Water** <note>Alchemical symbol for water</note> is the seed of all metals, which cleanses all things and also holds everything necessary within itself. Through this, the mastery is completed and the "dissolution of the Wise" arises, which turns bodies into a spirit and transforms the spirits back into ashes. For this reason, in our **dissolution** <term>Solution: the process of liquefying a solid substance to release its inner spirit</term> there is a tempered fire that does not alter the acting life-spirit or kill the **Soul** <term>Anima: the vital, transformative essence of the matter</term>. 

Thus should you govern your work, for Nature works toward the birth of metals solely through tedious and steady, tempered boiling, separating the pure from the impure with constant separation and evaporation. And the more often this work is performed with **putrefaction** <term>putrificiren: the stage of decomposition where the matter "dies" and turns black before its rebirth</term>, **dissolving**, and **solidifying**, the more precious this heavenly **Medicine** <note>The alchemist refers to the Philosopher's Stone as a "Medicine" for both metals and the human body</note> appears, and in its supernatural **virtue** <note>original: "virtute"</note> it **tinctures** <term>tingiret: to deeply permeate and transform the nature and color of a substance, such as turning lead into gold</term> all human and metallic bodies. Therefore, do not let this labor vex you, and you will truly enjoy this jewel, for in the **repetition** <term>Reiteration: the repeated cycles of distillation or refinement to increase the potency of the work</term> the **Powers** <term>Virtutes: the inherent strengths or medicinal properties</term> elevate themselves infinitely.

But that this happens and proceeds very slowly is because one must give Nature room and not attack her too harshly or quickly, but rather guard her until the noble color changes itself through daily and natural **purification** <term>Rectificiren: refining a substance to its purest state through gentle heat</term> with gentle fire, which Nature does in her own time and character. ### ...to the highest Medicine.

Our **dissolving** <note>original: *Solution*</note> of this beautiful green vapor <note>likely referring to the "Dunach" or green spirit from the previous page</note> is accomplished very slowly; for that which is brought back to its first origin requires time and great diligence, which is the entire foundation of this Art, until it can become such a subtlety of light in the **physical egg** <note>original: *ovo physico*; a hermetically sealed glass vessel used in alchemy</note>, which must be well **sealed** <note>original: *sigilliret*</note> and governed with a gentle fire until it has turned black. Then the **Azoth** <term>the universal life-force or "mercury" of the philosophers</term> is transformed into the **Mercury of the Sages** <note>represented by the symbol ☿</note>, and this is the first **degree of the fire of putrefaction** <note>original: *Gradus ignis putrefactionis*; the stage of decay and blackening known as *nigredo*</note>, which lasts for two months. After this, the **degree** <note>of heat</note> is raised a little for the **boiling** <note>original: *Decoction*</note>, and is gently governed and strengthened until it becomes a **coagulated mass** <note>original: *Coagulum*</note>; this is the second **degree in the governance of fire** <note>original: *ignis regimine*</note>. Then, **after total drying out** <note>original: *post exsiccationem totalem*</note>, the third **degree of the fire of sublimation** <note>original: *ignis sublimationis*</note> is given, so that the **coagulated** and white **matter** can **rise** <note>original: *eleviren*</note> in the **physical egg**, which is the **Physical Lunaria** <note>the white stage of the work, associated with the moon and silver</note>. The fourth **degree of reddening** <note>original: *rubefactionis*</note> is given for the **fixation** <note>the process of making a substance resistant to fire</note> and **purple-redness**; and if God gives you this, then you have the treasure of all **tinctures** <note>the transmuting agents</note> and the clarity of all Nature.

The more often it is thus **dissolved** and **coagulated**, the more its **power** <note>original: *Virtus*</note> is **exalted**, and it is then the greatest pillar of Nature, and herewith the mastery is accomplished:

Once you have placed the **Azoth** <term>the universal life-force or "mercury" of the soul</term> or **Mercury** <term>represented by the symbol ☿</term> of the Sages into the **vial** <term>the *viol* or glass vessel</term> so that one-fourth of the glass is filled, seal it well and place it in a gentle warmth, or **vapor bath** <note>original: *Balneum vaporosum*; a method of heating using steam to provide a steady, mild temperature</note>. This first subtle heat is like a brooding hen; when this heat is correctly directed, one can more easily ascend with it and maintain good governance, so that the external heat stirs the internal warmth of the matter without overpowering it; thus the Work is not easily ruined. Then allow it to **putrefy** <note>original: *putrificiren*; the stage of decay and breakdown</note> and **dissolve** <note>original: *solviren*</note>, which does not happen immediately, for nature requires its own appropriate time for the operation. The longer one allows it to putrefy, however, the more certainly the Work reaches its conclusion; for the **soul** becomes subtle through this process, so that by mixing with the **spirit**, it can rise beneath the height of heaven and unite eternally. For through such ascent and descent, our **Stone** <note>the Philosopher's Stone</note> is initially led to death, so that it may afterwards live eternally.

Therefore, be mindful of my warning, that you do not think to accomplish anything with haste or the swiftness of the fire, which does not happen without harm. For nature desires no haste or great heat; she wishes for her own convenient time and space to rule and to work, in order to ripen <term>mature</term> all that is perfect.

The gentle fire is now the <term>ferment</term> <note>in alchemy, a "ferment" is an agent that triggers a transformation in a larger mass, much like yeast in bread</note> in this divine art, and it is the greatest secret, which unites the <term>Azoth</term> <note>the "Universal Solvent" or essential agent of alchemy, believed to contain the beginning and end of all things</note> with its Fire [△], in such a union that it is nevermore destroyed or separated. For the Azoth is a body <note>original: *Corpus*</note> and spirit <note>original: *Spiritus*</note>, and is the entire composition itself of all elements and colors, of all bodies <note>original: *Corporum*</note> and principles <note>original: *Principiorum*</note>, from which the tingeing soul <note>original: *Anima*; the active, transforming essence</note> is prepared, which places the nature of all earthly things into the highest perfection.

And the entire art is also nothing other than the Azoth or the golden Water [▽], in which all bodies <note>original: *Corpora*</note> dissolve like ice in warm water; and this Water [▽] is called <term>quicksilver</term> <note>original: *argentum vivum*; the "living silver" or philosophical mercury, distinct from common liquid metal</note>, and is worked initially in a vaporous fire <note>original: *in igne vaporoso*; a gentle, moist heat similar to a steam bath</note>. And if this is converted through gentle boiling into a black earth <note>representing *Nigredo*, the first stage of the Great Work, signifying the death of the old form</note>, then its power is whole, and it is retained by the spirit <note>original: *Spiritu*</note> until the stability of perfect whiteness and redness <note>referring to *Albedo* and *Rubedo*, the final stages of the process that produce the White Stone and the Red Stone</note>. Then the superfluity of the moist nature is taken away and brought to dryness, and the invisible spirits incorpo-

...operates constantly towards the highest power and effect. Whoever understands this is a son of wisdom and eternal truth, through the teachings of philosophy <note>original: *Philosophia*</note>, whose name of eternal Wisdom be blessed.

Whoever now desires to be a true physician <note>original: *Medicus*</note> and a perfect chemist <note>original: *Chymicus*</note>, let him not begrudge the effort and cost, for he will not only bring out the value of what he has invested with the greatest profit <note>original: *cum maximo foenore*; a financial term used here to mean a spiritual and physical return on investment</note>, namely the noble Universal <term>Universal</term> <note>the Universal Medicine or Panacea, believed to cure all diseases and perfect all metals</note> and Tincture <term>Tincture</term> <note>a potent alchemical substance capable of transforming base metals into gold or restoring health</note>, but he will also see that which will delight and gladden him.
