### C H A P T E R VI.

## ON SPAGYRIC RECTIFICATION.
<note>original: *DE RECTIFICATIONE SPAGYRICA*. Spagyrics is the alchemical practice of separating a substance into its components and then recombining them in a purified state.</note>

Everything that grows also multiplies, and every creature brings forth its own kind; and everything that is ordained for perfection can be brought to perfection through our rectification <note>the process of refining or purifying a substance, often through repeated distillation</note> and washing in the sealed glass. Therefore, nature is indeed capable of being changed, so that from imperfect things, perfect ones may become. However, a separation and a casting away must occur of that which should not enter [the final work]; for the impure must be washed away so that the good which is within may become manifest and can appear in its clarity. For because of the curse which went over nature from God <note>a reference to the "Fall of Man" in Genesis, which alchemists believed left all of creation in a corrupted or "fallen" state</note>, nature has become very corrupted and deficient. Whoever can now take away such deficiency and curse, and come to the aid of nature, he is a right and true philosopher <note>original: *Philosophus*; in this context, specifically a "Philosopher by Fire" or alchemist</note>.

But no impure body <note>original: *Corpus*</note> can be dissolved <note>original: *solviret*; meaning to be broken down into its essential parts</note> and opened up; rather, it must be brought to the highest purity. Then, the gentle fire joins such things in the smallest parts <note>original: *per minima*</note> and inseparably with the **Azoth** <term>Azoth</term> <note>the "universal solvent" or "mercury of the philosophers," considered the essential agent of transformation and the beginning and end of the alchemical work</note>, and brings the innermost out into the outermost, and the hidden into the light. This is a pure, incombustible essence, the fixed light of nature, the radiance and

<meta>catchword: Form</meta>

<summary>This page introduces Chapter VI, focusing on the theory of Spagyric Rectification. It argues that the alchemist's role is to "heal" nature by removing the spiritual and physical impurities (the "curse") inherent in matter to reveal its inner, perfect light.</summary>
<keywords>Rectification, Spagyrics, Azoth, purification, corruption of nature, alchemy, philosophical light, dissolution</keywords>

The Form of the Metals, which enlightens and makes all bodies perfect; whoever does not know this Light of Nature wanders in manifold error, for to this light there is but a single way, by the inversion of the elements <note>original: *Umkehrung der Elementa*; the alchemical theory that the four elements must be rotated or transformed into one another to reach the quintessence</note>, for through this metamorphosis [transformation], that which is hidden is brought into the light.

Note well: But to prepare our Stone <note>The Philosopher's Stone</note> without the elements and their inversion, as the sophists <note>original: *Sophisten*; a term used by alchemists to describe charlatans or those who use false logic rather than true experimental knowledge</note> boast, is false; for solely through the inversion of the elements is this work brought to a praiseworthy end, and therein the black and imperfect Laton <term>Laton: an alchemical term for an impure metallic body or "base" metal, often associated with copper or brass, that must be purified</term> is washed of its destructibility and blackness, from which arises the Raven’s Head <note>original: *Raben-Haupt*; also known as the *Caput Corvi*, this signifies the *Nigredo* or the first stage of the work, characterized by blackness and the "death" of the matter</note>, through the separation of the blackness from the Mercury <term>Mercury (☿): in this context, the "Philosophical Mercury" or the transformative spirit of the matter</term> in the upper part of the vial. And this separation is the beginning of the Art; then the air and spirit penetrate the Laton with a sweet mildness and make it penetrating and spiritual, until various colors appear upon the Raven’s Head. When you have accomplished this, thank God for His blessing, for from it arises the perfect white Tincture and Medicine, which, with a small increase of the fire, elevates itself like a glistening snow within the glass, and it is the beautiful Lunaria <note>original: *Lunaria*; associated with the Moon (Luna) and Silver, this represents the *Albedo* or "Whiteness" stage, which was said to be able to heal the body and transmute metals into silver</note> of the Sages, and the first miracle-medicine of this secret operation.

This Whiteness arises from the Blackness through our rectification <note>the process of refining or purifying a substance through repeated distillation</note> and washing, that is,

### OBSERVATION.

Our art is a mirror of the secret **wisdom of God**, wherein the **resurrection of the dead** is clearly to be seen; to whom God grants this great treasure through His mild goodness, he is a spiritual son of the highest light, whose bright-shining rays are never extinguished, and he possesses the treasure that is more precious than all that may be called precious, and is elevated above all riches; therefore, pray to God for grace and the enlightenment of the **Holy Spirit**, that He may kindle the inner spirit with the sweet flame of love, so that we may persevere in true knowledge of God, and finally enjoy the joy of the elect.

For this burns the body more than a great fire does, because the body becomes very subtle through it, and transforms itself into a heavenly <term>Star</term> <note>original: *Astrum*; the quintessential or "astral" power within the matter.</note>

# ON SPAGYRIC
# COAGULATION.
<note>Spagyric refers to the alchemical process of "separating and recombining" substances to achieve a higher state of purity.</note>

The Philosophers say, **Fire and Azoth are sufficient for you in this spagyric art** <note>original: *Ignis & Azoth tibi sufficiunt in hac arte spagyrica*. However, when the Philosophers mention many preparations and elaborations, you must understand it thus: that in their **Universal** <note>The "Great Work" that applies to all of nature.</note>, Nature itself performs all such **operations** <note>Specific alchemical procedures</note> with our **Water** <term>▽</term> in the **physical egg** <note>original: *ovo physico*; the oval glass vessel used to simulate the conditions of creation.</note> with an external fire and warmth; for through this single means of heat, it dissolves itself, **refines** <note>original: *subtilisiret*</note> and coagulates itself, marries...

### CHAPTER IX.
## ON SPAGYRIC CALCINATION.

**Alchemy** is an art that teaches how to transform all kinds of metals into a better one, and is called the **introduction to a better form** <note>original: "Introductio ad meliorem formam"</note>. This must happen through its own spirit and powers, for no other thing is **sufficient** <note>Latin: "sufficient"; here meaning adequate or capable.</note> to improve the metals and transform them perfectly. Nor can anything cause a transformation except only that which has its beginning in and has sprung from our **Mercury** <note>original: "☿"; the fluid, passive, or "spirit" principle in alchemy.</note> and **Sulfur** <note>original: "🜍"; the combustible, active, or "soul" principle.</note>. For these may not be separated or divided; therefore we must work them and reach the lowest purification with them, through which it attains such high perfection, which is a thousand times nobler than what Nature prepares. This occurs through steady, enclosed heat **in a closed glass** <note>original: "in vitro clauso"</note>; this accomplishes everything in one vessel and open <gloss>furnace</gloss> <note>The original German "offen" (open) is clarified by the interlinear gloss "ofen" (furnace), indicating the heat source.</note>, and is the true **Calcination** <note>The process of heating a substance to high temperatures to change its physical state, usually to a fine powder.</note> of the Sages, which

### OBSERVATION <note>original: "OBSERVATIO"</note>

In this way, great things are accomplished—not only in the spiritual healing of people through a mere touch, but also many wonders performed in natural magic <note>original: "Magia naturali"; a branch of magic dealing with the hidden powers within nature and the elements rather than spirits.</note>.

## CHAPTER X.

## ON SPAGYRIC FIXATION.

This <term>fixation</term> <note>the alchemical process of making a volatile substance stable and resistant to heat</note> is nothing other than making a moist
spirit stable through natural fire in the <term>physical egg</term> <note>original: *ovo physico*; a hermetically sealed, egg-shaped glass flask</note>,
and thus drying it out gradually with a gentle
fire, so that the stable powers remain fixed in the spirit,
and penetrate wonderfully; for one <term>body</term> <note>original: *Corpus*</note>
cannot penetrate or <term>transform</term> <note>original: *tingiren*</note> another <term>body</term>,
but rather it is the moist spirit, fixed in the fire, which is
the driver; it must be the one to do it. If it is altered
and driven away by strong <term>fire</term> <note>original: the alchemical symbol Δ</note>,
then its <term>residue</term> <note>original: *Residuum*</note> is a carcass and <term>dead head</term> <note>original: *Caput mortuum*; the worthless, exhausted solid left after distillation</note>;
but when the <term>elements</term> <note>original: *Elementa*</note> remain together in

# True and Infallible Way

look upon the nature of this spirit and its own summer, and upon its own heaven, as if upon its proper autumn and season—the time in which it has reached its highest point through its own fire. Thus you may recognize by the time and its **astronomy** <term>Astronomia; here referring to the internal movements and celestial cycles of the substance</term> how it stands in its **exaltation** <term>Exaltation; the process of raising a substance to a higher state of dignity or potency</term> and how it will arrive at its **maturation** <term>Maturation; the final ripening or completion of the alchemical process</term>.

### OBSERVATION.

He who attains this great gift of God receives dominion over Nature; for he breaks the bonds of Nature and transforms the old age of man into youth, trees into greenness and constant fruit-bearing, and stones and **metals** <term>Metalla; the base metals to be transmuted</term> into the most noble essence.

### CONCERNING EXALTATION AND AUGMENTATION. <note>original: *De Exaltatione et Augmentatione*</note>

If one adds little ferment—the seventh or tenth part—then the work is completed through the perfect **solution** <term>Solution; the dissolving of a solid into a liquid state</term> over a long period of time;

### CHAPTER XII.
## ON THE PHILOSOPHICAL FIRE. <note>original: *DE IGNE PHILOSOPHICO*</note>

The first degree of fire <note>original: *Gradus ignis*</note> in our Work should be no higher than the warmth of a sitting hen, from which putrefaction <term>Putrefaction; the essential first stage of the alchemical process where the matter decays and turns black, allowing for its eventual rebirth</term> arises. Hereafter, take care to prevent the greatest harm: above all things, never increase the fire with haste. For fire <note>original uses the symbol Δ</note> is the sharp sword of the Cherub <note>A biblical reference to the flaming sword of the angel guarding the entrance to Eden; here it warns that excessive heat will "drive" the alchemist out of the successful completion of the Work</note>, so that you are not driven out of Paradise and the Hesperian Pleasure-Garden <note>In Greek mythology, the Hesperides guarded a garden of golden apples; alchemists used this as a metaphor for the successful completion of the "Great Work"</note> and lose all your labor and expense. For if the external heat exceeds the internal warmth of the **Azoth** <term>Azoth; the essential "mercury of the sages," considered the soul of all matter and the universal solvent</term> with too much speed, the Work is ruined.

Therefore, govern the fire <note>original: Δ</note> gently and steadily; then you will be certain and secure in bringing the Work to its perfect end. For when the internal warmth of the water <note>original uses the symbol ∇</note> is awakened by the external heat of the fire <note>original: Δ</note>, the **Azoth** has enough within its own nature to act, and to bring forth the highly praised fruit through its own power. This must be well observed in all operations <note>original: *Operationibus*</note>. In this way, the **King** <term>The King; a common alchemical symbol for the perfected, solar principle of the matter, often associated with gold or the final goal of the Work</term> rises and is transformed into a spirit—sharp and penetrating—and finally settles at the bottom of the glass in a white form. When you see this, do not hurry, but give it plenty of space at the end, so that the Work [may become] all the more perfect

This lily you shall pluck, and from it make a beautiful crown for your head, so that you may rejoice in this world; then you shall be perfect and have a great harvest.

This vessel must be well-coated with good lute <term>Lute; a clay or paste used to seal laboratory apparatus airtight and protect glass from direct flame</term>, and must be spherical in shape with a long, narrow neck of one ell <note>An old unit of measurement, roughly the length of a man’s arm</note> in height, so that the spirit of our mercury <note>original: *Spiritus ☿ nostri*</note> can rise and fall again according to the artist's will. In this way, one can observe the degrees of fire <note>original: *Gradus ignis*</note> and bring the Work forward conveniently and without harm. For the universal rule of Philosophy <note>Here meaning the "true philosophy" of alchemy</note> is this: Take care that in the governance of the fire you do not make it too strong; otherwise, you have no difficulty to fear in this matter.

### OBSERVATION.

With haste and excessive fire, the most subtle spirit of life or **soul** <note>original: *Anima*</note> is killed in our work; for a forced drying does not **tinge** <term>Tingeing; the alchemical power of the Stone to instantly transform and "color" base metals into gold or silver</term>. Indeed, the **Medicine** <note>The alchemical Elixir or "Philosopher's Stone" in its medicinal form</note> is also killed by the **fire** <note>original: Δ, the alchemical symbol for fire</note> and turned into a poison. This is because strong fire turns even the sweetest honey or sugar into a corrosive and poison during distillation; for nature suffers nothing forced, much less any violence, which causes suffocation. Therefore, the **artist** <note>The alchemist/practitioner</note> must assist this wise worker <note>Referring to Nature</note> only with steady warmth, so that she may give birth without hindrance and bring her fruits to ripeness.

Where the union in our art occurs in the proper manner, and the **governance of fire** <note>original: *Regimen ignis*</note> is well directed, the work proceeds happily. But if someone takes too much of the **Azoth** <term>Azoth; the essential "universal solvent" or mercury of the philosophers, believed to be the transformative agent of the Great Work</term> into the **vial** <note>original: *Viol*</note>, he not only drowns the seed, but it is also impossible to **fix** <note>original: *figiren*; to turn a volatile, liquid substance into a solid, stable state that can withstand fire</note> it in the **fire** <note>symbol: Δ</note> due to the long time required. But if you take too little, the **body** <note>original: *Corpus*; the solid material or metal being worked upon</note> could not **dissolve** <note>original: *solviren*</note>, but would wither and could bring no fruit; therefore you must maintain the right proportion, so that you reach a good end. ### to the highest Medicine.

**coagulate** <note>to solidify or fix a substance through heat</note> it in hot ashes several times, and then it is the **drinkable gold** <note>original: *aurum potabile*</note> of the metallic and human bodies, the highest Medicine that exists on Earth, and the highest **Tincture** <note>the concentrated essence used for transformation</note> of the world; also, from this hour, 1 part of such a Tincture can be cast upon a thousand parts of **raw or common quicksilver** <note>original: *☿ vivicrudi seu vulgi*</note> when it smokes in the crucible, and it will become a red powder; of this, take again 1 part and cast it upon a thousand parts of **quicksilver** <note>original: *☿*</note> kept warm in the crucible, and it will once more become Tincture and red powder. Continue this casting-on until the smoking **mercury** <note>original: *☿*</note> in the crucible no longer turns to powder and Tincture, but into pure **gold** <note>original: *☉*</note>.

Therefore, it is better that this unspeakable gift of God or Tincture be first made into a **drinkable gold** <note>original: *Auro Potabili*</note> or **Oil** <note>original: *Oleo*</note> before the **projection** <note>the act of throwing the alchemical agent onto base metal to transform it</note>. Let it be **dissolved** <note>original: *solviret*</note> in a **water bath** <note>original: *Balneo*; specifically the Balneum Mariae</note> and **coagulated** <note>solidified</note> in ashes. With this Oil, **soften** <note>original: *incerire*; to bring to a waxy consistency</note> another Tincture-powder in the warmth, within a **luted** <note>sealed with airtight clay or paste</note> wide glass, until it becomes like a **gum**. When it cools, cast some of this wet mass [onto the metal], for it will then be easily absorbed; but if the Tincture is cast on while dry, much of this noble and unspeakable Tincture is lost before it can be integrated, because of its extreme purity. If it is dissolved into an Oil, however, it performs the highest work in both human and metallic bodies.

# True and Infallible Way

From this **drinkable gold** <note>original: *Auro potabili*</note> or **dissolved** <note>original: *solvirten*</note> Tincture, take 1 drop in 4 quarts of pure and cold well- or flowing water; swirl and **incorporate** <note>original: *incerire*; to blend thoroughly until the substance reaches a waxy or unified state</note> it well with the water in a wide, clean glass or **alembic** <note>a pear-shaped distilling vessel</note>, until you no longer perceive even the slightest bit of **Oil** <note>original: *Oleum*</note> or Tincture. Then, let 8 ounces of **well-purified quicksilver** <note>original: *☿ vivum bene purgatum*</note> flow into it, and let it stand still and quiet. After 5 or 6 hours, you will see with the greatest amazement, arising **from the globe of mercury** <note>original: *ex globo mercurii*</note> and out of the water, a strange **wonder-growth** <note>original: *Wunder-Gewächs*</note> and a beautiful **golden little tree** <note>original: *güldenes Bäumlein*</note> or shrub, which **extends** <note>original: *extendiret*</note> its small twigs and very delicate branches up above the water, appearing and emerging at the very top as fine as a hair. The **mercury** <note>original: *☿*</note> is **transformed** <note>original: *mutiret*</note> in its **essence** <note>original: *Essenz*</note> and entirely **consumed** <note>original: *consumiret*</note>, and according to its **weight** <note>original: *Pondus*</note> it is divided into this little tree with its branches and twigs; this growth occurs visibly in the glass with the highest joy and greatest wonder. When the **mercury** <note>original: *☿*</note> has completely **transformed** <note>original: *mutiret*</note> into this growth, then take it delicately out of the glass and preserve it as a great **rarity** <note>original: *Rarität*</note>.

And again, after swirling the water, let 8 ounces of **purified quicksilver** <note>original: *☿ vivi purgati*</note> flow in, and you will see with astonishment that this same **mercury** <note>original: *☿*</note> also, like the previous one, visibly emerges from the water into this golden growth and like—

# True and Infallible Way

It is upon this that the *Philosophers* <term>true seekers of alchemical and divine wisdom</term> have looked, when they wished to compare the mastery of Nature to the Creator—a mastery which lies hidden not in *dung & urine* <note>original Latin: *Stercore & Urina*</note>, but in the Earth, where the *Spirit of the universe* <note>original Latin: *Spiritus universi*</note> communicates itself to all *creatures*, and forms itself bodily into our *Virgin Earth* <note>original Latin: *Terra virginea*</note>. The Creator took this *virgin & spiritual earth* <note>original Latin: *terram virgineam & spiritualem*</note> as a pure, beautiful, and bright body for Himself, and drew forth the spiritual, clear, and purest essence that was located in this *Chaos* <term>the primal, unrefined state of matter</term> as if from a hidden place and made it manifest. In doing so, He made from it a *creature* that was to be similar to God’s image, namely Man, by building him from the *Quintessence* <term>the "fifth essence," the purest and most potent spiritual force</term> of the earthly *Olympus* <note>the "earthly Olympus" refers to the highest, most refined spiritual peak of the natural world</note>. This is now the virgin earth from which the first man was taken; it consists of the Spirit and Soul of the Creator, which is represented on the Earth by the purest and brightest **Salt**, and this spiritual earth reveals itself in the *Hesperian* Garden <note>the mythical garden of the Hesperides, where golden apples grew; here used as a metaphor for the alchemical paradise</note> as a pure and beautiful Virgin in her greening *habit* <note>garb or clothing</note> to all those whom God has deemed worthy to understand the high mystery of Nature. For in this Earth, the *Matter* of the Great Work is to be found, because it encloses within itself the <term>Sulfur</term> and <term>Mercury</term>, that is, the *Soul* and the *Spirit*. # True and Unerring Way

Now take this *virgin Earth* <note>original: *Terram virgineam*</note> or *Electrum* <note>an alchemical alloy or primary mixture of metals</note>, purify it to the highest degree, and draw from it the *Azoth* <term>the universal solvent or essential spirit of mercury</term> or *Mercury* <note>represented by the symbol ☿</note> with a gentle, vaporous fire. This is the *viscosity of the Sages* <note>original: *Viscosa*</note>. Place it in a *philosophical egg* <term>a pear-shaped glass maturation vessel</term>, seal the glass, and set it in the *athanor* <term>a furnace designed to provide steady, prolonged heat</term> in gentle warmth until it turns black. This is the *impregnation* <note>original: *Schwängerung*</note> and the key to this art, for the male acts upon the female, the *Sulphur* <note>represented by the symbol 🜍</note> acts upon the *Mercury*, and they bring forth a highly praised fruit. 

When, through gentle cooking, the blackness passes away and the colors appear, the *fixed* <note>the solid, stable element</note> acts upon the *moist* <note>the volatile, fluid element</note> until it completely overcomes its power and the matter becomes white. Afterwards, the *Phoenix* reveals itself, taking on the highest redness as a sign of its fiery nature. Then such *Medicine* is heavenly and perfect, through which all the *filth of the metallic bodies* <note>original: *Sordes*</note> is washed away, and the *hidden things of the metals* <note>original: *Occulta metallorum*</note> are made manifest; for it penetrates everything and makes it perfect through its virtue.

which is moved and perfected by the external fire; that is the form of the Art, that the external fire <note>original: Δ, the alchemical symbol for fire</note> should give its heat in the Athanor <note>a slow-burning furnace used by alchemists to maintain a consistent temperature over long periods</note>, just as the sun in the world gives light and life to the stars and elements; likewise, the external fire in the Athanor (and not in the <margin>bracketed in red ink</margin> wood-burning stove) gives to our <term>proximate matter</term> <note>Latin: *proxima materia*; the immediate substance prepared for the final stages of the Great Work</note> life and movement, perfection and ripening, the highest virtue and formation.

But one thing you must note: you shall not hasten in your philosophical work, as many ignorant sophists <note>false alchemists who used trickery or lacked true understanding</note> do, who wish to prepare the <term>Stone</term> <note>Latin: *Lapidem*; the Philosopher’s Stone</note> in nine days in a tiled stove; or like the false artists who wish for the Son of the Sun to be born in nine months or a year, like a grain of corn or a human in the mother’s womb. For the more noble a thing's nature is, the more perfectly it is generated, and the more time it requires for its growth; for whatever comes to be and grows quickly also perishes quickly. Grain and humans are born quickly, therefore their lives are also short and transient. # True and Infallible Path

**PLUTO**, the first artist and prince of all the others, carries a yellow flag; his sign is ☉ <note>The astronomical symbol for the Sun, representing Gold in alchemy</note>, and the motto on his flag reads: *Every man shall reap the sown seeds of his own art.* <note>original: *Quisque suae artis semina jacta metet*</note> This rich artist treats Gold <note>original: ☉</note> *through the excellence of his art* <note>original: *per artis excellentiam*</note>, and brings these fixed, fire-resistant, and beautiful bodies into such quality, subtlety, and volatility that it is beyond description; so much so, that one might blow the high tincture of the wise in a single hour with the help of *Vulcan* <note>The Roman god of fire; here referring to the intense heat of the bellows and forge</note>. However, because he is the most distinguished and highest artist, he demonstrates his high spirit better than the others, and shuts his refined, volatilized, and qualified *Phoenix* <note>The mythical bird that rises from its own ashes, symbolizing the process of chemical renewal and purification</note> into the *philosophical egg* <note>original: *ovum physicum*; a sealed glass vessel used in alchemical heating</note>, where it shall burn, consume itself, and multiply infinitely. Meanwhile, he has it proclaimed throughout his entire kingdom: Anyone who wishes to profit tremendously with him and attain a supernatural tincture, *for transmuting all metals into the purest gold* <note>original: *ad transmutandum omnia metalla in purissimum solem*</note>, should bring a good portion of the best gold <note>original: ☉</note> and deliver it to him in his art-chamber and secret cabinet. As soon as he has received this, the *preparation* begins, the *calcination* <note>the process of reducing a metal to a fine powder through intense heat</note> follows, the *solution* shows itself, and the *putrefaction* <note>the stage of "rot" or decomposition, usually marked by the matter turning black</note> is clear; the colors begin to shift and darken so that the interested party sees it for themselves and can grasp it with their hands, because it is *demonstrated to their eyes* <note>original: *ad oculos demonstritet*</note> and *placed into their hands* <note>original: *ad manus insinuiret*</note>. Thus, there is now the greatest hope and certainty of all...

### Concerning the Highest Medicine

left alone, whereby **Vulcan** <note>original: *Vulcanus*. A shallow, porous bowl used in the process of cupellation to separate precious metals from base metals like lead or tin.</note>.

# True and Unerring Way #

"Come, black and white **Astrogen** <note>Likely a made-up name for a spirit or substance, combining 'astro' (star) and 'gen' (born).</note>, increase my money; let it happen now by right or wrong <note>original: *per fas & nefas*</note>, through sulfur or arsenic, through poison or vapor, through smoke or stench." The fifth and last chief sophist of the Plutonian realm is *VULCANUS*, the smith-god and laborer for all the others. ->Wahrhaffter und untrüglicher Weg<-
<note>True and Infallible Way</note>

From the **limbs** <note>original: "Membris." In an alchemical context, this refers to the constituent parts or members of a substance.</note>, however, they wish to extract the **salt, sulfur, and mercury**, and then artistically reassemble them to bring them to a **tincture** <term>Tincture: A potent alchemical preparation intended to transform or "tinge" base metals into gold or to act as a universal medicine.</term>. For to first divide that which **GOD** and **Nature** have joined together, and only then attempt to bring it to multiplication and improvement—that is worthless. It is no different than if an experienced surgeon, in order to heal someone and put them in a better state, were to have his patient's arms and legs, head, hands, and feet chopped off, only to reassemble them later to heal and restore the whole body more "skillfully," even though the entire recovery could have been accomplished with a simple plaster or a medicinal **spirit**. 

In such an irrational manner do the **sophists** proceed in all their matters and operations. In their high "spirit of folly," they wish to be more skillful than the Creator and Nature herself. Their **skulls** <note>original Latin: "Cranium"</note> are out of alignment, and their brains are inflamed with nothing but hot spirits that they hope will bring them pure gold and silver from **Ophir** <note>Ophir is a biblical region famous for its wealth and high-quality gold; here used to mock the alchemists' greed.</note>. <meta>This page continues the warning from page 170 regarding the futility of "sophistical" alchemical methods. ### On the Highest Medicine.

...then afterward sow such a thing; what fruit would you hope to harvest from it? Namely, none at all, but only toil and labor, expense, folly, and public shame. This is exactly how it would go for you with such **sophists** <note>In this context, a "sophist" is a "false alchemist" or charlatan who uses trickery rather than the true laws of nature.</note>; you would lose your money along with your precious time, and continually work in vain.

Therefore, as the sincere **Hermogenes** <note>A common pseudonym in alchemical literature, likely referencing Hermes Trismegistus, the legendary founder of the Hermetic arts.</note>, I wish to warn you, my lover of the true Art, that you should not approach any sophist who does not operate and mature according to nature. Rather, as soon as you have learned and discovered his <term>subject</term> <note>original: "Subjectum," the base material chosen for the alchemical work.</note> or <term>matter</term> <note>original: "Materie"</note> from which he works and intends to make something grand and extraordinary—and once you have inquired of him his <term>method of proceeding</term> <note>original Latin: "Modum procedendi"</note> only in general terms—if it involves casting, melting, distilling, cementing, sublimating, and fixing, without natural <term>maturation</term> <note>original: "Maturation." In true alchemy, "maturation" refers to a slow, gentle ripening of the matter, similar to a fruit on a tree or a child in the womb, rather than violent chemical reactions.</note>, then dismiss that fool to his proper banner in the **Plutonic** and **Vulcanic** <term>laboratory</term> <note>original: "Laboratorio." These terms refer to the use of "violent" fire (Vulcan was the god of the forge, Pluto the god of the underworld), which the author considers destructive to the "vital seed" of the metal.</note>. Let him roast, sputter, and toil there until he has sublimated his foolish wits so high that he loses his place and falls into the dregs. Keep your gold and money in your purse, and rather do something good for your own body with it; in that way, you will at least have enjoyed it.

I will give you an example of this: suppose a cunning, crafty sophist came to you and persuaded you, or tried to convince you, that he was a

-># True and Infallible Way<-

...were a great artist <note>In this context, "artist" refers to a practitioner of the alchemical art.</note> and possessed the secret of the **Great Tincture** <note>Also known as the Philosopher’s Stone, the "Tincture" was the substance believed to be capable of "tinging" or transforming base metals into gold.</note>, which could transform and **transmute** all metals into the purest and finest gold; then simply ask him what he requires and must have for it.

In alchemy, "flowers" refer to the powdery or crystalline flakes that form on the neck of a flask during sublimation (heating a solid until it becomes vapor and then solid again).</note>; and if he intended to produce the metallic tincture by these means, which should improve <note>original: "melioriren"</note> other metals and endlessly "tinge" <note>original: "tingiren," the act of the Stone's quality penetrating and changing a base metal.</note> them in **exaltation** <note>original Latin: "exaltatione," a process by which the power or grade of an alchemical substance is heightened.</note>—

—even if he were to swear so solemnly that his eyes might pop out of his head, do not believe a single word of it. Instead, promptly assign <note>original: "promovire"</note> him to the yellow banner of the great **Sophist** <note>A "sophist" in alchemical literature is a charlatan or a "false brother" who uses deceptive logic and complex chemistry to hide their lack of true natural knowledge.</note>, **Pluto**, as the god of wealth and a great "gold-artist." and provides an advance for his alchemy <term>Gold-Kunſt: literally "Gold-Art," the practice of transmuting metals</term> *from the rich cavern of Pluto* <note>original Latin: *ex ditis Antro Plutonis*; Pluto was the Roman god of the underworld, associated with the mineral wealth found deep within the earth</note>, from his great and wealthy treasury; and keep your money in your purse for better **fortune**. For gold is a ripe fruit, grown and ripened from the great mineral tree of Nature. Now, one certainly cannot graft the ripe fruit onto the trunk and root of a tree; rather, it must be a good little branch if it is to thrive, grow, and bring forth fruit. ->### True and Unerring Path ###<-

[So]phist were to take it for the Art <term>Art: referring to Alchemy, often called the "Hermetic Art"</term>, he would raise a bizarre tree, for he would have to place the tree's fruit into the earth and turn the root outward and upward; what kind of fruit would you hope to have from such a tree? Therefore, reflect, my lover of the Art—even if you were ever so rich and had gold in abundance—on my warning: that one can become poor and unhappy if the **desire for gold** *extends* <note>original: *extentiret*; to stretch or expand</note> itself too far in your mind.

Likewise, dismiss that sophist who labors in common *Mercury* <note>original: *Mercurio*; the metal quicksilver</note> and wishes to *fix* <note>original: *figiren*; to stabilize a volatile substance into a solid</note> it into gold and silver with various sophisticated *labors* <note>original: *Laboribus*; chemical operations or experiments</note> and manipulations; it is all false and the stuff of fables. Even if he could bring all *metals* to *running Mercury* <note>original Latin: *ad Mercurium currentem*; mercury in its liquid, mobile state</note>, including gold and silver, it remains but a *running Mercury* and a sophisticated, vain labor. But should he say that this <term>☿</term> *running [Mercury]* was the beginning of the great *Tincture* <note>The "Tincture" or Philosopher's Stone was the goal of the Great Work, believed to "tinge" or transform base metals</note> and the first *Principle* of all metals from which they originated, and that from this <term>☿</term> the *Philosophers’* <term>☿</term> <note>The symbol ☿ represents Mercury; "Philosophers' Mercury" was a specialized, secret substance distinct from common quicksilver</note> must be *prepared*, as it is said: *make Mercury through Mercury, and you shall have the tincture of metals* <note>original Latin: *fac Mercurium per Mercurium, & habebis tincturam metallorum*</note>—that is, "turn the running Mercury into our philosophical Mercury, and you will obtain the great Tincture of all metals"—as soon as you hear such things and see him working from them...

# Regarding the Highest Medicine #

[...per]sist <note>The text begins mid-word, likely completing *bestehest* (persist or maintain)</note> [in this], then send him to the underworld of Pluto <note>original Latin: *ad orcum Plutonis*; Pluto was the Roman god of the underworld</note>, to Lord General Satan <note>original: *Sahatan*</note>, the wind-maker and smoke-seller. This arch-sophist and most subtle devil and deceiver in all chemical operations <note>original: *chymischen Operationibus*</note> will certainly set him straight on how he should behave if he wants to practice the craft correctly; indeed, he will inform and instruct him on how he should turn his subject <note>original: *Subjectum*; the material being acted upon</note> and Mercury <term>☿: the symbol for Mercury or quicksilver</term> into the first matter <note>original Latin: *prima materia*; the primordial, formless base of all substance</note> of all creatures. He will tell him how the world was created from its water and how, to this day, it derives its growth and life from it. He will impress <note>original: *imprimiren*</note> this upon him as firmly as if it were the pure truth and the very Gospels <note>original: *Evangelia*</note>.

For he deceives all laboratory workers <note>original: *Laboranten*</note> in the whole world and promises them wind and smoke instead of gold and silver. You should dismiss such a man and his foolish wisdom, saying: "If it were true that this subject was the Mercury of the world <note>original Latin: *Mercurius mundi*</note> from which all things originated, would it then also be the same one that made the great mountains green and fertile, adorned the meadows with the most beautiful flowers and lilies, and clothed all forests, fields, and gardens with the most magnificent fruits?" At this, he will fall silent and have no answer at all, for he is utterly defeated by this argument. Furthermore, you can convince him of his brutish ignorance <note>original: *Ignoranz*</note> and deceit when you say: "If this is the right Mercury <term>☿</term> for the Art <term>Art: Alchemy, the "Hermetic Art"</term>, then it must—

<meta>This page continues the author’s polemic against fraudulent alchemists. On the previous page, the author argued that if the common "mercury of the world" were indeed the base of all life as the "sophists" claim, its raw, volatile state would be incredibly dangerous.</meta>

...[would] travel through the whole earth and penetrate even into the stars—from which may **GOD** protect us. For before it reached the surface of the earth <note>original Latin: *ad superficiem terræ*</note>, all creatures—humans and cattle, birds and fish, trees and herbs—would have to perish and be ruined forever through its smoke and poison. That would be a fine "Spirit of the round world" <note>original Latin: *Spiritus mundi rotundi*; a theoretical alchemical substance believed to permeate the universe</note> for me! Who indeed would remain to create the tincture from it in order to drive away such a great universal plague? <note>original: *Universal-Pest*</note> Not even a dog, let alone a human.

With this, you can now, my lover of truth, drive away the lying spirits, deceivers, and sophists of Pluto <note>original Latin: *Plutonis Sophisten*; Pluto was the god of the underworld, here associated with hell and greed</note>. You can expose them before the honorable world, reveal their shame and deceit, and banish them to the underworld of Pluto <note>original Latin: *ad orcum Plutonis*</note>. Let them bubble this "Universal Spirit" and "Mercury of fools" <term>Mercurium stultorum: a derogatory term for common quicksilver when used incorrectly by amateur alchemists</term> in hell. Let them boil it down into a "lung-stew" <note>original: *Lungen-Gemüsse*; a metaphor for a worthless or foul concoction</note> and syrup for all five head-sophists and black devils, until it becomes a "tincture" and "universal" <note>The "Universal" refers to the Panacea or Philosopher's Stone</note> in the underworld of Pluto (*in orco Plutonis*). 

There, Satan <note>original: *Sahatan*</note>, the supreme artist and arch-sophist, can tincture all the dragons, worms, and snakes found in hell, and transmute <note>original: *transmutiren*</note> them into nothing but mercurialists, petty-foggers <note>original: *Rabulisten*; those who use deceitful or trivial arguments</note>, and sophists. In this way, his many-colored mercurial banner may shine all the more brightly as it draws such an army of head-laborers <note>original: *Laboranten*; those who work in alchemical laboratories</note> after it.
